### INC-028 Gate-1 reviewer amendment: refusal granularity, non-mutation, and heal serialization
- date: 2026-08-04 / status: accepted contract correction before Gate 2 / triggered_by: independent review of INC-028 Gate 1 at `2695f5f` / related: INC-028, DEC-054, DEC-060, modelark/fetch.py, modelark/fill.py, tests/test_inc028_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md, tests/test_inc028_gate1_contracts.py
- refusal_granularity: `APPROVED_TARGET_DIGEST_MISMATCH` remains a run-terminal integrity conflict. `APPROVED_TARGET_DIGEST_UNPROVABLE` is instead a typed per-requirement follow-up: Fetch records it, continues unrelated approved repositories, and Fill parks only the affected requirement after directing the operator to DEC-054 repair. It must not consume the generic retry budget or claim `PLAN_SATISFIED`; completion with otherwise safe work is `PLAN_COMPLETE_WITH_FOLLOWUPS` carrying the exact content refusal.
- non_mutation: Both pre-download classifications preserve the archived row and existing worktree bytes. Refusal performs no deletion, overwrite, metadata update, upsert, or download.
- heal_serialization: Wiring `heal_replica_archived_from_source` makes its formerly unreachable race live. Gate 2 must serialize the source/target read and mutation with `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, and null-target fill must be a compare-and-set guarded by `orig_sha256 IS NULL`. A contradictory concurrent non-null digest must never be overwritten, satisfying DEC-060's halt-before-mutation rule.
- scope_boundary: This amendment changes expected-red contracts and durable bookkeeping only. It does not authorize Gate 2 production implementation.
- UPDATE 2026-08-04 — A second independent Gate-1 pass supersedes the `refusal_granularity` sentence that made `APPROVED_TARGET_DIGEST_MISMATCH` run-terminal. Both mismatch and unprovable states are localized, typed per-requirement follow-ups: the affected repository halts before download or mutation, while unrelated frozen approved repositories continue. Their remedies remain distinct—inspection/disposition for mismatch, DEC-054 repair first for unprovable—and neither may produce `PLAN_SATISFIED`. Gate 1 additionally pins approved-digest comparison against raw SHA256E-derived evidence and requires `fill._archive_content_satisfies` plus `execution_projection._file_content_satisfied` to delegate to the single pure `archive_hash.content_satisfies` implementation. No Gate-2 production is authorized by this update.
- UPDATE 2026-08-05 — Final Gate-1 review hardening pins successful replica-heal commit visibility from a fresh connection, mismatch rollback/lock release, the exact mismatch action prefix, and coexistence of access-gated plus content-refusal evidence in one `PLAN_COMPLETE_WITH_FOLLOWUPS`. The consolidation boundary is explicit: `content_satisfies` answers only whether supplied archived evidence satisfies supplied approved content; `expected_sha256` remains the resolver used directly by restore, verification, and repair code that asks what digest evidence exists. Those resolver callers must not be folded into the approval predicate. Contracts and bookkeeping only; Gate-2 production remains separately authorized.
- UPDATE 2026-08-05 — Reviewer-authorized Gate-1 correction supersedes `c08`'s first-occurrence ordering assertion, which became unsatisfiable once `c02b` required initial work derivation to delegate through `content_satisfies`. The replacement requires a post-Fetch predicate call in every inter-attempt window and after the final attempt, preserving the intended proof that durable satisfaction is re-derived after each Fetch return while permitting mandatory pre-Fetch derivation. Retry bounds and terminal assertions remain unchanged. This contract-only correction does not alter Gate-2 authority or production scope.
- UPDATE 2026-08-05 — **Gate 2 production implemented at `05ab8fa`; pending CI and reviewer acceptance.** The pure scalar-only `archive_hash.content_satisfies` predicate is now the single approval-content rule used by projection, Fill, proposal completion, explicit-manifest resume, and replica decisions. Present mismatched or unprovable approved targets produce distinct typed per-requirement follow-ups before download or mutation; unrelated approved work continues. Fetch and replica completion are re-derived from exact durable evidence after each attempt rather than inferred from transport aggregates, with bounded zero-progress terminals. Replica healing is wired under `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, guarded compare-and-set, commit visibility, and rollback-on-conflict. Gate-2 evidence: frozen contracts 22 passed; full non-E2E suite 804 passed, 5 warnings; Ruff on every changed file and `git diff --check` clean. One baseline authority test fake was corrected to persist the durable archive evidence its mocked successful fetch reports; the frozen INC-028 contract file was not modified by production. No schema, migration, live catalog/policy reopen, repair, Fill, drive, selection, PR-state, or merge action occurred. Production remains under review and is not frozen by this update.
- UPDATE 2026-08-05 — **Gate-2 remediation wording correction:** supersedes DEC-060 §3 and the remediation handoff §3.6 wherever they describe a merely "null target digest." Replica metadata healing may fill only when the target has **no resolvable original-byte digest evidence** under `expected_sha256(catalog_sha=None, orig_sha256, compressed, annex_key)`. A null `orig_sha256` column whose raw SHA256E annex key resolves to a contradictory digest is a contradiction and must halt before mutation; column nullness alone never authorizes fill. This correction authorizes no live repair, migration, Fill, drive operation, Batch B work, PR-state change, or merge.
- UPDATE 2026-08-05 — **Gate-2 remediation implemented at `2690a0d`; pending CI and reviewer acceptance.** Replica heal decisions now pass source and target scalar evidence through one pure classifier using the canonical resolver/predicate semantics; the transactional writer no longer compares a narrower raw-column view. A raw SHA256E target contradiction halts without metadata mutation or false completion, and the caller re-reads committed evidence and re-verifies content rather than trusting a heal status string. `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` is inside a `BaseException` rollback envelope with rollback failure unable to mask the original interrupt. Content refusals now retain `PLAN_COMPLETE_WITH_FOLLOWUPS` when same-repository replica requirements wait on the parked primary, while unrelated residual waits remain explicit evidence. Remediation evidence at implementation tip: 24 INC-028 contracts passed; full non-E2E suite 806 passed, 5 warnings; legacy DEC-060 heal matrix and gated-retry budget regression passed; injected `KeyboardInterrupt` released the transaction; Ruff and `git diff --check` clean. No live operation or §10.7-excluded work occurred; production remains under review and is not frozen by this update.
- UPDATE 2026-08-07 — **Gate-2 F1 wording correction supersedes the overly absolute 2026-08-05 "may fill only when the target has no resolvable evidence" sentence.** Replica healing has three safe enrichment cases under the shared resolver: (1) no resolvable target evidence plus a usable source column digest permits digest-and-provenance fill; (2) independently resolvable target evidence equal to the source plus a NULL target digest column permits the same digest-and-provenance enrichment; (3) an already-populated matching target digest permits provenance-only fill. Matching key-only evidence on both sides with no source column digest is satisfied without metadata mutation. Any resolved disagreement still halts before mutation. Provenance is never written while `orig_sha256` remains NULL. This correction authorizes no live operation or work excluded by §10.7.

### INC-033: Resolver precedence hides disagreement between a digest column and raw annex-key evidence
- date: 2026-08-07 / status: active — recorded for a later evidence-consistency gate / triggered_by: INC-028 Gate-2 remediation review at `fe7e9e2` / related: DEC-053, DEC-054, DEC-055, DEC-060, INC-028, modelark/archive_hash.py, modelark/fetch.py, modelark/hash_repair.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- symptom: `archive_hash.expected_sha256` prefers a populated `orig_sha256` over a raw SHA256/SHA256E annex key, so a row whose two independently resolvable evidence fields disagree is treated according to the column and the internal contradiction is not surfaced. The current migration and repair contracts do not establish whether such rows exist outside synthetic reproduction.
- risk: Content satisfaction or replica healing can accept the preferred digest while ignoring a contradictory key. This is pre-existing resolver behavior and is not introduced by the INC-028 F1 fix, but it must be measured and explicitly dispositioned before live repair, Fill, or provenance cutover relies on those rows.
- boundary: Do not change resolver precedence or expand INC-028 remediation to cross-evidence reconciliation. Inventory the fleet and design a separate fail-closed contract gate. No live catalog query, migration, repair, Fill, drive operation, Batch B work, PR-state change, or merge is authorized by this entry.

### INC-028 Gate-2 F1 remediation: Digest and provenance enrichment are one classified mutation
- date: 2026-08-07 / status: implemented at `9d3bf86`; pending CI and reviewer acceptance / triggered_by: Gate-2 remediation review at `fe7e9e2` — matching raw SHA256E target evidence took provenance-only fill while `orig_sha256` remained NULL / related: INC-028, INC-033, DEC-053, DEC-055, DEC-060, modelark/fetch.py, tests/test_inc028_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md, tests/test_inc028_gate1_contracts.py
- resolution: The pure replica-heal classifier now names `fill_kind=digest|provenance`; the transactional writer executes that classified mutation rather than reinterpreting `fill` from resolved evidence. A matching raw-key target with a NULL digest column and a usable source column digest receives digest and source provenance together under the existing evidence-guarded CAS. Provenance-only fill requires an already-populated matching target digest. Matching key-only evidence with no source column digest remains satisfied without mutation, and resolved disagreement still halts.
- evidence: Red-first contract tip `b3cf025` produced exactly 2 new failures with 24 prior contracts passing: matching-key integration ended `(NULL,hub_confirmed,key)` and the pure classifier exposed no fill kind. Production `9d3bf86` passes 26 INC-028 contracts plus the historical DEC-060 heal matrix; full non-E2E suite 808 passed, 5 warnings; Ruff and `git diff --check` clean. Exact-head CI and reviewer acceptance remain required.
- boundary: INC-033 is recorded but not implemented here. No live catalog, repair, migration, Fill, drive, selection, Batch B/delivery-gap, PR-state, or merge action occurred.
- UPDATE 2026-08-07 — **Gate 2 accepted; production frozen at `d457a1b`.** Supersedes every earlier INC-028 statement that production or remediation remained “pending CI and reviewer acceptance,” including the Gate 2 implementation update at `05ab8fa`, the remediation update at `2690a0d`, and the F1 remediation entry at `9d3bf86`. Independent whole-PR review accepted Batch A / INC-028 Gate 2 (content satisfaction, completion accounting, heal classifier/writer split, post-heal re-verification, interrupt envelope, FOLLOWUPS preservation, and F1 atomic digest+provenance enrichment) at exact tip `d457a1b22480b87bfbfbbea1c3f3c6e9736ca579`. Acceptance evidence: 26 INC-028 contracts passed; full non-E2E suite 808 passed, 5 warnings; GitHub Actions run `31174506109` green on that exact head for `e2e` / `test (3.10)` / `test (3.12)`. Production code for INC-028 is **frozen** at `d457a1b`. Further INC-028 production changes require new operator authorization and a new gate cycle. This acceptance is Gate 2 only: it does not authorize Gate 3, PR #55 ready/merge, Batch B (INC-029), INC-030–033 implementation, DEF-035/036, or any live migration, repair, Fill, drive, or selection operation.

### INC-029 Gate-0 UPDATE: SQLite bundle publication, immutable rehearsal capture, and measured classification
- date: 2026-08-08 / status: Gate 0 inventory complete; pending reviewer acceptance; Gate 1 not authorized / triggered_by: operator authorization in remediation handoff §23.2 / related: INC-012, INC-029, DEC-053, DEC-057, DEC-059, modelark/core/db.py, scripts/migrate_legacy_runtime.py, tests/test_dec053_054_gate1_contracts.py, tests/test_dec053_054_gate2_remediation.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- finding_partition: **A / Finding 2 is an unconditional merge blocker**: publication can place a validated main database beside a foreign destination `-wal`, `-shm`, or `-journal`. **B / Finding 6 is a cutover blocker**: rehearsal can mutate the stopped canonical source while recovering its hot WAL. **C / Finding 8 is report-honesty work carried with B**: the report must consume the migration's measured classification rather than synthesize it. Keep all three in one INC-029 gate cycle, but preserve these blocking classes in contracts and handback.
- publication_inventory: `publish_provenance_migration` (`modelark/core/db.py:1678`) is the sole DEC-059 publication entrypoint. Its only destination cleanliness check is `dest_cat.exists()` at `:1711`; no production helper enumerates the `catalog.sqlite` bundle or rejects destination sidecars. A future guard belongs (1) immediately after resolving `dest_cat`, before remigration/staging, and (2) after all validation and rollback-artifact work, immediately before `os.replace` at `:1873`, while source, clone, and staging locks remain held. The exact published path must then be revalidated for migrated `user_version` and the rehearsed `clone_content_identity` without opening an interval in which an unverified destination is writable. `_catalog_snapshot_metrics_con` and `_assert_publication_matches_expected` already provide semantic/row validation over an open retained connection; `_resolve_rehearsal_layout` already treats report paths as untrusted. Neither currently decides destination bundle cleanliness.
- quiescence_boundary: Publication's `source_lock` opens the **canonical** source and acquires `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` at `db.py:1722-1729`, retaining it through `os.replace` and releasing it only in `finally`. This is the authoritative no-concurrent-writer proof. `test_publish_holds_source_lock_through_replace_blocks_writer` already pins it. INC-029 must not redirect, shorten, or substitute this lock with a lock on a copy; rehearsal immutability and publication quiescence are different constraints.
- rehearsal_inventory: `rehearse_provenance_migration` (`db.py:1232`) first reads metrics through `_catalog_snapshot_metrics` using `mode=ro` plus `query_only`, then opens the canonical `catalog.sqlite` read-write at `:1267` solely to run `src_rw.backup(snap)` at `:1271`. Closing that last recovery-capable handle can checkpoint/delete a stopped-writer hot WAL. The source manifest is captured only afterwards at `:1277-1283`, so it can describe post-mutation main/WAL/SHM state. Rehearsal is called only by tests and `scripts/phase4_wheel_migration_smoke.py`; ordinary `db.connect()` merely directs operators to it, and the installed-command gap remains DEF-035. No production helper presently copies the stopped canonical main+WAL+SHM bundle before any recovery-capable open. `_manifest_entry` can describe the pre-copy artifacts. `scripts/migrate_legacy_runtime._backup_sqlite` is the closest sibling: it retains a canonical `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, uses SQLite backup, and preserves raw main/WAL/SHM copies, but its canonical read-write guard means it does **not** satisfy rehearsal's stricter source-immutable shape and must not be reused unchanged.
- disagreement_inventory: `_apply_provenance_backfill` (`db.py:1033-1082`) computes and returns all four counts, incrementing `disagreement` at `:1064` and raising if nonzero. `_migrate_provenance_v7` calls it at `:1136` and discards the returned dict; rehearsal calls that migration at `:1307`, then independently requeries three classes and writes literal `"disagreement": 0` at `:1325`. `_migrate` and `_remigrate_snapshot_to_expected` are sibling callers that do not consume a report. The future report path must thread the actual returned classification; `test_m06_disagreement_refuses` remains the independent fail-closed pin for a real nonzero disagreement.
- sibling_inventory: `scripts/migrate_legacy_runtime.execute` refuses the entire destination directory if it exists and publishes a staged directory with `os.replace`, so an absent directory is its cleanliness authority; that shape is not directly reusable where `publish_provenance_migration` intentionally accepts an existing empty directory. `_backup_before_migration`, `_remigrate_snapshot_to_expected`, clone/staging SQLite backup, `_full_catalog_identity`, and `_assert_publication_matches_expected` already provide consistent-copy and semantic-validation pieces, but none copies a canonical hot-WAL bundle before opening it or rejects foreign destination sidecars. Existing `test_m08c_existing_destination_refuses`, `test_publish_staging_exclusive_lock_blocks_adversary_through_replace`, and the source-lock test remain green boundary characterizations rather than substitutes for INC-029 contracts.
- proposed_gate1_contracts: In a new `tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py`, all new cases are expected-red against this Gate-0 tip. **Merge class A:** `test_a01_publish_refuses_preexisting_destination_sidecar_before_staging` (parameterize WAL/SHM/journal; preserve sentinels; no staging or replace); `test_a02_publish_rechecks_destination_sidecars_immediately_before_replace` (inject after initial guard; refuse before replace; preserve source/clone/rollback evidence); `test_a03_publish_revalidates_exact_destination_path_before_releasing_staging_lock` (exact `user_version` and clone content identity; injected foreign WAL or rival writer cannot produce `status=ok`; retained lock excludes the writer through verification). **Cutover class B:** `test_b01_hot_wal_rehearsal_preserves_exact_canonical_bundle_and_prework_manifest` (crash-style committed WAL, exact main/WAL/SHM bytes and presence unchanged, manifest equals the pre-rehearsal bundle, snapshot/clone contain WAL-resident data); `test_b02_rehearsal_never_opens_canonical_source_recovery_capable` (poison any non-read-only canonical open; copied bundle may be recovered; rehearsal succeeds with matching logical identity). **Report class C:** `test_c01_rehearsal_report_consumes_backfill_returned_classification` (inject a distinctive returned count dict and require the report to preserve it rather than requery/literal-zero). Retain the existing canonical publication-lock and real-disagreement refusal tests unchanged.
- scope_boundary: Gate 0 produced inventory and contract names only. It authorizes no Gate 1 test file, production edit, schema change, live rehearsal/publication/migration, INC-030–033 work, DEF-035/036 delivery work, Gate 3, PR ready/merge, repair, Fill, physical-drive action, or selection mutation. Gate 1 requires separate operator authorization after review of this inventory.

### INC-029 Gate-1 UPDATE: Migration-boundary contracts implemented expected-red
- date: 2026-08-09 / status: Gate 1 contracts implemented; pending reviewer acceptance; Gate 2 not authorized / triggered_by: operator authorization in remediation handoff §27 / related: INC-029, DEC-053, DEC-057, DEC-059, modelark/core/db.py, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py
- contract_evidence: Six named contract functions produce nine behavior-specific expected-red cases against unfixed Gate-0 tip `82cae34`: A01 is parameterized across destination `-wal`, `-shm`, and `-journal`; A03 covers both a post-replace foreign sidecar and rival main-file replacement. The remaining A02, B01, B02, and C01 cases each fail on their own observable defect. No missing production helper is used as a shared red trigger.
- publication_contracts: A01 requires a sidecar-only destination to refuse before staging or replace while preserving each sentinel. A02 injects a sidecar after the first guard and requires a second refusal immediately before replace, with rehearsal evidence and rollback artifact retained. A03 attacks the destination pathname inside the replace seam and forbids success for either a foreign sidecar or a rival main, requiring exact-path identity and sidecar cleanliness to be revalidated while the staging lock remains held.
- rehearsal_contracts: B01 creates a committed hot WAL in a subprocess that exits via `os._exit`, then requires canonical main/WAL/SHM bytes and presence to remain exact, the manifest to describe that pre-work bundle, and the clone to contain the WAL-resident row. B02 poisons every recovery-capable open of the canonical source while allowing read-only canonical inspection and copied-bundle recovery; rehearsal must still succeed and retain the WAL-resident row.
- report_contract: C01 runs the real backfill under a spy that returns distinctive measured hub-confirmed, legacy-unknown, and null-digest counts with disagreement zero; both returned and on-disk reports must consume that dict. Existing `test_m06_disagreement_refuses` independently owns the nonzero halt path.
- evidence: `.venv-dev/bin/pytest -q tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py` reports exactly **9 failed** on the intended assertions; the four retained boundary tests report **4 passed**; Ruff and `git diff --check` are clean. No production file, schema, live catalog, rehearsal/publication/migration, repair, Fill, drive, selection, PR-state, or merge operation changed or ran. Gate 2 production and every later queue item remain separately authorized.

### INC-029 Gate-1 operator amendment: Exact pre-lock publication rollback bundle
- date: 2026-08-10 / status: contract implemented expected-red; pending reviewer acceptance; Gate 2 not authorized / triggered_by: operator rejection of treating publication-time hot-WAL normalization as a documentation-only residual / related: INC-029, DEC-053, DEC-059, modelark/core/db.py, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py
- decision: Publication may retain its authoritative canonical `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` from validation through replace, even though SQLite can checkpoint the canonical hot WAL while acquiring/releasing that lock. This is acceptable only after publication has preserved an **exact, verified pre-lock rollback bundle**. Physical canonical-file immutability is not promised; exact recoverability plus locked logical identity is required and enforced, so the prior §26.2 default of a documentation-only residual is superseded.
- rollback_bundle_contract: New B03 creates a stopped hot WAL whose final user-table identity still equals the rehearsal. Before any recovery-capable canonical open, publication must retain `rollback/source-bundle.pre-publish/` containing exact `catalog.sqlite`, `catalog.sqlite-wal`, and `catalog.sqlite-shm` bytes plus `manifest.json`. The manifest records source and rollback paths, presence, size, SHA-256, `status=validated`, and the independently recovered source content identity. Recovery/validation runs only on a disposable copy; the retained bundle remains byte-identical. The locked canonical identity must still match the recovered bundle and rehearsal evidence before replacement.
- outcome_contract: B03 is parameterized across normal publication success and a post-replace foreign-WAL refusal. The same validated exact bundle must survive either outcome. Together with A03, a late destination attack cannot produce success or erase rollback evidence.
- evidence: The amended Gate-1 file has seven named contract functions and **11 expected-red cases** at production-unchanged tip `b49a648`: the B03 success case reaches successful current publication and then fails because no exact bundle manifest exists; the late-refusal case reaches the injected post-replace attack and fails because current publication still reports success. All nine prior cases retain their original defect-specific reds. Ruff and `git diff --check` are clean.
- baseline: The unchanged suite excluding the expected-red Gate-1 file was re-run after the amendment and remains **808 passed, 5 warnings**.
- scope_boundary: This amendment changes contracts and append-only bookkeeping only. It authorizes no production/schema change, live or operator-catalog operation, Gate 2, INC-030+, DEF-035/036, INC-033, Gate 3, PR-state change, or merge.
- UPDATE 2026-08-10 — **INC-029 Gate 2 production implemented; pending CI and reviewer acceptance.** Publication refuses destination `-wal`/`-shm`/`-journal` before staging and immediately before `os.replace`, and refuses residual sidecars or destination-path inode drift after replace while the exclusive staging lock is retained. Rehearsal copies exact main/WAL/SHM bytes into a disposable capture directory and never opens the canonical path recovery-capable; the pre-work manifest is frozen from those bytes. Publication captures `rollback/source-bundle.pre-publish/` (exact bytes + validated recoverable identity) before the canonical `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, then requires locked logical identity to match the bundle and rehearsal evidence; the bundle is retained on success and late refusal. `_migrate_provenance_v7` returns measured backfill counts and rehearsal reports them without requery/literal-zero. Evidence: 11 INC-029 contracts passed; migration-related suites 69 passed; full non-E2E 819 passed, 5 warnings; Ruff and `git diff --check` clean. No live catalog, Gate 3, PR ready/merge, or INC-030+ work.

### INC-030 Gate-0 UPDATE: Explicit repair-drive archive-resolver wiring inventory
- date: 2026-08-10 / status: Gate 0 inventory complete; pending reviewer acceptance; Gate 1 not authorized / triggered_by: operator authorization in remediation handoff §34 / related: INC-030, DEC-054, DEC-057, DEC-060, modelark/cli.py, modelark/hash_repair.py, modelark/register.py, tests/test_dec053_054_gate1_contracts.py, tests/test_dec053_054_gate2_remediation.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- cli_inventory: The installed `modelark` entrypoint (`pyproject.toml:59`) dispatches `repair-drive` to `cli.cmd_repair_drive`. Its sole production call to `hash_repair.run_explicit_drive_repair` is `modelark/cli.py:242-247`; it passes the connection, drive, identity epoch, and fingerprint but omits `archive_resolver`. No other production caller invokes the explicit engine. The smallest future wiring is the existing callable `register.archive_path`, not a second resolver or a default invented inside the explicit engine.
- accessor_inventory: `register.archive_path(con, label: str) -> Path | None` (`modelark/register.py:499-507`) reads `drives.fs_uuid`; it returns `None` for an unknown/unregistered label, missing UUID, unresolved block device, or unmounted device, otherwise `<mountpoint>/modelark` as a `Path`. Its `blkid`/`lsblk` probes use `check=False`, and a missing platform tool degrades to empty output/`None`. Database-query and other unexpected `Exception`s can still escape the accessor; `run_explicit_drive_repair` catches `Exception` from the supplied resolver and treats it as absent. It does not catch `BaseException`. The accessor does not certify that the returned archive directory or stored file exists; tier-2 validation owns strict path and Git-object checks.
- engine_inventory: Identity and obligation checks precede resolver I/O. Lost lifecycle with zero archived rows returns `obligation=False` without a resolver call (`hash_repair.py:567-579`); invalid/mismatched fingerprint or epoch halts first (`:581-596`). For eligible work, a supplied resolver is called once for the named drive at `:600-610`; `None` or an `Exception` sets `drive_absent=True`, while a path enables tier-2 at `:625-632` only after tier-1 annex-key resolution fails. `_validate_candidate` errors leave the row unresolved; valid archive-head evidence is checked against Hub evidence and disagreement halts atomically before the planned CAS updates.
- status_inventory: After repair, zero unresolved rows are `complete`, including an absent drive whose rows were fully solved without drive I/O. With unresolved rows, `drive_absent` plus lifecycle other than `lost` is `blocked_absent` / `archive_unavailable` (`:661-663`); lifecycle `lost` is instead `needs_refetch` / `lost_drive_unresolved` (`:664-666`); a present active drive with unprovable rows is `needs_refetch` / `unresolved=N`. Therefore wiring `register.archive_path` fixes the active-unmounted mislabel without changing lost-drive or tier-3 semantics.
- sibling_inventory: `audit_hashes` already selects `archive_resolver or register.archive_path` at `hash_repair.py:205`; `repair_hashes` calls that audit before and after its backup/lock, and CLI `repair-hashes` therefore reaches the shared accessor even though it passes no explicit resolver. Tests also pass focused lambda resolvers. These are reusable precedents, but `run_explicit_drive_repair` intentionally consults no resolver when its argument is `None`, so the generic audit default must not be mistaken for coverage of `repair-drive`.
- test_inventory: Existing `test_w08_cli_exact_engine_args` proves CLI dispatch and exact drive/epoch/fingerprint but never inspects `archive_resolver`, so it is false-green for INC-030. Direct-engine `test_w04_archive_head_git_fixture` proves tier-2 with an explicit path; `test_w15_blocked_absent` proves active absent with an explicit `None`; `test_w03_tier1_complete`, `test_w12_lost_unresolved_not_complete`, `test_w13_needs_refetch_exact`, `test_w14_lost_drive_zero_archives_no_repair_obligation`, and `test_w17_fingerprint_halt_no_archive_mutation` cover adjacent state branches. Gate-2 tests further pin wrong epoch, missing/invalid fingerprints, identity races, and tier-1/tier-2 Hub disagreement. None joins the installed CLI to `register.archive_path`, tier 2, or active-absent disposition. `tests/test_hash_repair.py` covers the separate generic audit/apply surface whose resolver already defaults correctly.
- proposed_gate1_contracts: In new `tests/test_inc030_gate1_contracts.py`, expected-red against this Gate-0 tip: `test_c01_cli_repair_drive_passes_register_archive_path` (spy the engine and require the exact callable by identity); `test_c02_cli_resolver_path_reaches_archive_head_tier2` (real CLI/engine, accessor returns a Git archive, persist digest with `archive-head-blob` provenance); `test_c03_cli_resolver_none_active_drive_records_blocked_absent` (real CLI/engine, active unresolved drive, durable `blocked_absent` / `archive_unavailable`, never `needs_refetch`); `test_c04_cli_wrong_epoch_receives_resolver_but_halts_before_resolver_io` (real engine through CLI, resolver is wired but never invoked, status `halted`, archive rows unchanged). Retain unchanged the direct-engine tier/status/identity tests above; specifically preserve lost-unresolved `needs_refetch` and lost-zero no-obligation behavior rather than broadening `blocked_absent` to every unavailable drive.
- scope_boundary: Gate 0 changed append-only bookkeeping only. It authorizes no Gate-1 test file, CLI/engine/accessor production edit, live repair or catalog access, INC-031/032, DEF-035/036, INC-033, Gate 3, PR-state change, ready/merge, Fill, migration, drive, or selection operation. Gate 1 requires separate operator authorization after review.

No engine, lifecycle, status, identity, schema, or accessor logic changed. All four INC-030 contracts pass; seven retained tier/lifecycle/identity boundaries pass; the full non-E2E suite reports 823 passed, 5 warnings; Ruff and `git diff --check` are clean. ### INC-031 Gate-0 UPDATE: Bound Dismiss error-body and Refusal-envelope inventory
- date: 2026-08-17 / status: Gate 0 inventory complete; pending reviewer acceptance; Gate 1 not authorized / triggered_by: operator authorization in remediation handoff §4 at tip `7547988`; Codex catch-up review confirmed Finding 7; Greptile catch-up on the same tip did not restated this residual / related: INC-031, DEC-058, modelark/web/static/app.js, modelark/web/static/fill.js, modelark/web/static/catalog.js, modelark/web/selection_api.py, modelark/web/server.py, modelark/web/fill_worker.py, modelark/proposal.py, modelark/execution_session.py, tests/test_e2e_portal.py, tests/test_blocked_selection_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- confirmed: Reviewer pre-review §3 is accepted with one amendment: the Gate-2 shape is **both** client error-body discrimination **and** translating `proposal.Refusal` in `selection_api.bulk` into the existing typed refusal dict. Client-only would close the toast/re-preview UX, but would leave the named DB-session residual as an untyped HTTP 500; the existing `_PreviewStale` catch and `_selection_result` 409 mapper are the siblings that already solve this class of envelope. Server rollback remains correct.
- client_inventory: `MA.post` (`app.js:8-12`) is `fetch(…).then(r => r.json())` — HTTP status is ignored; any parseable JSON is a resolved promise. `dismissBlocked` (`fill.js:655-702`) on that resolved body checks only `r.refused`; any other body, including `{error: "FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE: …"}`, falls through to the DEC-058 success re-preview (`MA.api("/api/plan/preview")`). `loadBlockedPreview` (`fill.js:631-636`) already treats `p.ok === false && p.error` as failure and retains notice evidence — that is the sibling discriminator Dismiss omits.
- transport_siblings: `catalog.js:101-111` documents the same `MA.post` status-ignore and uses `guardRefused` (`res.refused` only) on catalog checkbox removals. That sibling is **out of INC-031** unless Gate 2 chooses to change `MA.post` itself. 403 Origin, 403 CSRF, 415 Content-Type, 400/411/413 body, and the POST `except Exception` 500 envelope (`server.py:229-253`, `:299-300`) are all `{error: …}` without `refused` and are therefore misclassified by Dismiss today.
- guard_inventory: `_guarded` (`selection_api.py:64-69`) calls `fill_worker.WORKER.guarded_mutation`; a live **process-local** controller returns `None` → `_FILL_ACTIVE_REFUSAL` (`refused: True`, `code: FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE`). `_selection_result` (`server.py:122-126`) maps any `refused` dict to HTTP 409. That path is already correct and is pinned by `test_bs12_live_fill_bound_dismiss_fill_session_active_exact` plus e2e catalog 409.
- refusal_envelope: `selection_api._with_revision` (`:100-111`) calls `proposal.graph_write`. `graph_write` (`proposal.py:110-131`) raises `Refusal("FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE", live_owner, …)` when `execution_session.live_session_exists` sees a `starting`/`running`/`stopping` row and the connection is not session-write authorized. `bulk` (`selection_api.py:233-236`) catches only `_PreviewStale`. An uncaught `Refusal` becomes `server.py` POST `except Exception` → HTTP 500 `{"error": "FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE: …"}` with no `refused`. `Refusal.__str__` is `"{code}: {evidence!r}"` (`proposal.py:67-69`).
- test_inventory: E2E blocked-selection covers Replan GET-only, network abort, PREVIEW_STALE 409 (`refused: true`) retain-evidence, and successful Dismiss auto-re-preview. E2E catalog-removal 409 covers typed `FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE` on `MA.post("/api/selection")` via `guardRefused`, **not** Dismiss. No fixture posts Dismiss a 500/`{error}` body without `refused`. `bs12` pins the WORKER path only.
- chosen_gate2_shape: (1) In `dismissBlocked` `.then`, treat a body with `error` (and/or non-success semantics) as failure: toast, retain displayed blocked-notice evidence, do **not** re-preview. Do not require a shared `MA.post` status check unless Gate 1 chooses optional c04. (2) In `bulk`, catch `proposal.Refusal` the same way `_PreviewStale` is caught and return `_FILL_ACTIVE_REFUSAL` (or an equivalent `refused: true` / `code: FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE` body) so `_selection_result` emits 409. Do not invent a second Fill-guard; do not unify WORKER with `live_session_exists` (broader #39 residual).
- proposed_gate1_contracts: New `tests/test_inc031_gate1_contracts.py`, expected-red against this Gate-0 tip: `test_c01_db_live_session_bound_dismiss_is_typed_409` (real HTTP `/api/selection/bulk` with DEC-058 bindings; portal WORKER **not** running; `execution_sessions` has a live row; expect HTTP 409 + `refused` + `code=FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE`; selection rows and revision unchanged — today this is HTTP 500 `{error}` without `refused`). Playwright residual, in `tests/test_e2e_portal.py` blocked-selection harness (not a second browser stack): `test_c02_dismiss_error_body_toasts_and_does_not_repreview` (fulfill bulk as HTTP 500 `{error: "FILL_SESSION_ACTIVE: …"}` without `refused`; toast; notice evidence retained; zero additional preview GET). Regression: `test_c03_dismiss_typed_refused_still_toasts` (existing PREVIEW_STALE / typed FILL body still toasts and retains evidence). Optional `test_c04_ma_post_surfaces_http_error_json` only if Gate 1 chooses to change `MA.post`.
- scope_boundary: Gate 0 changed append-only bookkeeping only. It authorizes no Gate 1 test file, no JS/server/selection production edit, no live portal, INC-032/033, INC-034 production, DEF-035/036, Gate 3, PR-state change, ready/merge, Fill, migration, drive, or selection operation. Gate 1 requires separate authorization after review of this inventory.

### INC-034: Publication silently overwrites a late destination main file
- date: 2026-08-17 / status: active — recorded for its own gate; not a reopen of INC-029 / triggered_by: Codex catch-up review of `82e3f90..7547988` at tip `7547988`; independently reproduced the same session / related: INC-029, DEC-059, modelark/core/db.py, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- symptom: `publish_provenance_migration` refuses an already-present destination `catalog.sqlite` once, then later rechecks only SQLite sidecars and calls clobbering `os.replace(staging, dest_cat)`. A rival destination main file created after the first guard is overwritten and publication returns `status="ok"`.
- root_cause: The no-overwrite promise is a TOCTOU `Path.exists()` check (`db.py:1880-1884`), not an atomic no-clobber publish. The INC-029 second guard (`_refuse_destination_sidecars` at `:2064-2065`) does not recheck the main file. `os.replace` on Linux replaces an existing dest inode. Post-replace inode checks then validate the publisher's own staging inode, so the overwrite is reported as success.
- blast_radius: Destructive to an unrelated destination catalog. Source rollback bundle (`rollback/source-bundle.pre-publish/`) does not protect that dest file. Live publication is still unreachable (DEF-035); this is a cutover / merge residual, not a reason to reopen accepted INC-029 sidecar, hot-WAL, rollback-bundle, or classification contracts.
- why_not_caught_earlier: `test_a01`/`test_a02` pin sidecars; `test_a03` pins a rival **after** replace. No contract injects a rival **main** file between the initial `exists()` and `os.replace`.
- planned_remediation: Atomic no-clobber publish on the already-required same filesystem (for example `os.link(staging, dest_cat)` then unlink staging, or `RENAME_NOREPLACE`); keep fail-fast `exists()` and sidecar/inode checks. Expected-red contract: inject rival main after `_remigrate_snapshot_to_expected`, require refusal, byte-identical sentinel, no successful overwrite.
- boundary: Do not reopen INC-029. No live publication, rehearsal, or destination write is authorized by this entry.

### INC-034 Gate-0 UPDATE: Destination no-clobber inventory
- date: 2026-08-17 / status: Gate 0 inventory complete; pending reviewer acceptance; Gate 1 not authorized / triggered_by: operator instruction to inventory the Codex/Greptile catch-up residuals in the same cycle as INC-031 Gate 0 / related: INC-034, INC-029, DEC-059, DEF-035, modelark/core/db.py, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- helper_inventory: Fail-fast occupier check is `dest_cat.exists()` at `publish_provenance_migration` (`db.py:1878-1884`). Sidecar helper is `_refuse_destination_sidecars` (`:1180-1187`), called before staging, on the staging path, immediately before replace, after replace, and before success return. Publish primitive is `os.replace(str(staging), str(dest_cat))` at `:2073-2074`. Same-filesystem is already required (`st_dev` checks at `:2012-2021`). No existing helper performs a no-clobber rename; `os.link` on that already-same device, failing on `EEXIST`, is the portable sibling — not a second `exists()` check in front of `os.replace`.
- sibling_inventory: INC-029 A01 refuses pre-existing dest sidecars; A02 injects a late sidecar after `_remigrate_snapshot_to_expected` and requires `os.replace` not to run; A03 attacks the dest path **after** replace (foreign sidecar or rival main) and refuses success on inode/sidecar drift. None of those preserve a late-arriving dest **main** file. Isolated reproduction at tip `7547988`: inject v99 `rival_sentinel` after remigrate → `publish_status='ok'`, `rival_survived=False`, dest `user_version=7`.
- greptile: Catch-up review of tip `7547988` did not report this race. Its one new P1 (`tests/test_pr10_gate1_contracts.py:469-473`, post-entry typed exceptions satisfying the DEC-052 immutability pin) is a measurement-contract tightness item and is queued with INC-032, not INC-034.
- proposed_gate1_contracts: New `tests/test_inc034_gate1_contracts.py`, expected-red against this Gate-0 tip: `test_c01_late_rival_main_is_refused_and_preserved` (real `publish_provenance_migration`; inject a sentinel dest `catalog.sqlite` after `_remigrate_snapshot_to_expected`; require `RuntimeError`; dest bytes unchanged; staging must not have replaced the sentinel). Do not weaken A01–A03 or B03.
- scope_boundary: Gate 0 changed append-only bookkeeping only. It authorizes no Gate 1 test file, no `db.py` production edit, no live rehearsal/publication/migration, INC-031 production, INC-032/033, DEF-035/036, Gate 3, PR-state change, ready/merge, Fill, repair, drive, or selection operation. Gate 1 requires separate authorization after review. INC-034 does not block the already-authorized INC-031 Gate 0/1 cycle.
- UPDATE 2026-08-19 — **INC-034 Gate 0 accepted-as-amended at tip `f9ab8b6`.** Reviewer + Codex ×3. Defect stands. Binding amendment: remigrate-hook inject is not the sole Gate-1 pin (a second `exists()` would false-green). Greptile targeted pings were **partial** (DEC-061). Gate 1 authorized under handoff §3.

### INC-034 Gate-2 UPDATE: Destination no-clobber publication
- date: 2026-08-20 / status: Gate 2 production implemented; pending reviewer acceptance / triggered_by: operator instruction to address INC-034 after Gate 1 contracts at `5a4f5a2` plus Codex ×3 (ACCEPT / AMEND / ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED) / related: INC-034, INC-029, DEC-059, DEC-061, modelark/core/db.py, tests/test_inc034_gate1_contracts.py, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md, modelark/core/db.py, tests/test_inc029_gate1_contracts.py, tests/test_dec053_054_gate1_contracts.py, tests/test_dec053_054_gate2_remediation.py
- production: `publish_provenance_migration` publishes via `_publish_staging_no_clobber`: `os.link(staging, dest_cat)` then `os.unlink(staging)`. `EEXIST` refuses without overwriting a late dest main. Hard-link unsupported (`EXDEV`/`ENOSYS`/`ENOTSUP`/`EOPNOTSUPP`) and `EPERM` fail closed with distinct messages; never falls back to `os.replace`. Unlink failure after a successful link raises, leaving `staging` named so outer cleanup can retry. Fail-fast `dest_cat.exists()`, sidecar guards, same-filesystem checks, and the EXCLUSIVE staging lock through post-publish inode/sidecar validation are retained.
- contracts: c01 greens at the publish syscall (wraps `os.replace` and `os.link`; sentinel dest main preserved). A01–A03 / B03 / m08c keep their assertions; hooks now observe `os.link` as well as `os.replace`. m08d/m08e and the staging/source lock characterizations retarget the same primitive.
- evidence: focused INC-034/INC-029/m08c–e/lock contracts 17 passed; publication/migration suites 70 passed; full non-E2E 826 passed, 5 warnings after the unlink-failure and EPERM-message amendments. Codex pass 1 ACCEPT, pass 2 AMEND (swallowed unlink + EPERM wording), pass 3 ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED. Ruff and `git diff --check` clean on touched files.

### DEC-061: A Greptile green check without an answer is a partial review
- date: 2026-08-19 / status: accepted; remaining PR #55 Greptile use superseded by DEC-062 / triggered_by: operator instruction on PR #55 comment 5338007077 after INC-034 Gate 0 targeted pings (5337864936, 5337886599, 5337907947) returned only a green Greptile Review check / thumbs-up and restated INC-032 / related: DEC-057, INC-034, INC-032 / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- decision:
  1. A Greptile run that completes green (or thumbs-up) but does **not** answer the targeted question — no inline on the named files, summary still the prior whole-PR writeup — is a **partial review**, not a disposition.
  2. Do not treat that badge as “looked at and found nothing.” Fall back immediately to **local procedure**: this-seat review plus Codex. Stop spending further Greptile pings on the same unanswered question in that gate (cap still applies; unused slots are unused).
  3. Flag the operator on the next turn that Greptile was partial and that local evidence is what the gate used.
- rationale: INC-034 Gate 0 asked three times whether `publish_provenance_migration` can `os.replace` over a late dest main. Greptile never commented on `db.py`; it restated the queued INC-032 drain/config-skip items. Waiting on a fourth ping would have been the same non-answer. DEC-057 already forbids inferring gate evidence from a badge; this entry names the Greptile-specific form of that failure.
- impact: INC-034 Gate 0 disposition stands on local review + Codex ×3 (ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED). Future targeted Greptile pings follow this partial-review rule. No production change.
- scope_boundary: Process only. Authorizes no production, no Greptile product change, no gate skip of local/Codex review.

### DEC-062: Omit Greptile from remaining PR #55 residual gates
- date: 2026-08-20 / status: accepted / triggered_by: operator instruction after INC-034 Gate 2 Greptile ping 1 (comment 5357570766, check 96469501271) completed green on `2a48f51` while restating INC-032 / related: DEC-057, DEC-061, INC-032, INC-034 / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- decision:
  1. Do not request, wait on, or iterate Greptile for remaining PR #55 residual gates (INC-032, INC-033, DEF-035/036, Gate 3 bookkeeping).
  2. Gate review for those residuals is **local + Codex**. A Greptile green check is not a disposition.
  3. DEC-061 remains the rule if Greptile is ever used again on this repository: a green check with no answer to the targeted question is partial, not “looked at and found nothing.”
- rationale: Targeted pings on this draft PR repeatedly returned the queued whole-PR writeup (`execution_benchmark.py` / `tests/test_pr10_gate1_contracts.py`) instead of answering the named dest-main question. The operator judged the PR too large for Greptile to stay on-target. Local + Codex already closed INC-034 Gate 2.
- impact: Implementer handbacks and reviewer dispositions no longer include a Greptile loop for this PR. Does not skip local or Codex review. Does not mark ready, merge, or authorize live ops.
- scope_boundary: Process only for the remainder of PR #55. Authorizes no production, no INC-032 start by itself, no Gate 3, no PR-state change.

### INC-032 Gate-0 UPDATE: Acceptance-harness skip vs fail inventory
- date: 2026-08-20 / status: Gate 0 inventory complete; pending reviewer/operator acceptance; Gate 1 not authorized / triggered_by: operator go after INC-034 Gate 2 accept at `57da6bf` / related: INC-032, DEC-052, DEC-062, modelark/execution_benchmark.py, modelark/wishlist.py, tests/test_pr10_gate1_contracts.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- finding_partition: **A / Finding 9 + config-skip P1** is resolver honesty: `except Exception: cfg = {}` around `wishlist.load()` maps YAML/IO/type errors to `(None, "acceptance_fixture_path_absent")`; `run_acceptance_wall_clock` then returns `ok=True` / `skipped_measurement=True` when the descriptor omits `sqlite_path` and `project_fn is None`. **B / drain-exception P1** is a loose **test** outcome tuple, not a production swallow: after `drain_hits >= 1`, `test_dec052_measure_refresh_leaves_evidence_bytes_unchanged` accepts `ValueError|RuntimeError|OSError|sqlite3.Error`. Keep A and B in one INC-032 cycle.
- helper_inventory: Sole path resolver is `resolve_acceptance_fixture_path` (`execution_benchmark.py:28-49`); keys `fixture_sqlite_path` / `sqlite_path`; no env. Genuine absence is `(None, "acceptance_fixture_path_absent")`; missing file is `(None, "acceptance_fixture_path_missing")`. Skip recording is `run_acceptance_wall_clock` `skipped_measurement` + `skip_reason`. Invalid fixtures already fail closed via `validate_acceptance_fixture_descriptor` / `_reject_synthetic_org_m_fixture` (`Refusal`). Config load is `wishlist.load()` (`wishlist.py:47-48`) with no catch; `cli.py` lets it propagate. **Missed then adopted:** `wishlist.acceptance()` (`wishlist.py:92-96`) is the existing DEC-052 acceptance mapping (defaults `{fixture_sqlite_path: None}`, `update(load().get("acceptance") or {})`); load/type failures propagate; the resolver bypasses it. Drain entry is `_measure_executor_refresh_boundaries_on_copy` `wrapped_drain` (`:933-935`); the outer `measure_executor_refresh_boundaries` only copy-first delegates.
- sibling_inventory: `test_dec052_fixture_path_from_config_not_env_and_skips_when_absent` uses `config={}` which is falsy and still calls `wishlist.load()`; in this tree `wishlist.yaml` has `fixture_sqlite_path: null`, so it skips without hitting `except Exception` and does **not** pin load-failure. Keep it. DEC-052: typed skip for genuine absence; never synthesize. Finding 9: let load errors surface. `compression()`/`download()` are the same defaults+section pattern as `acceptance()`.
- reproduced: at `57da6bf`, `config={"fixture_sqlite_path": None}` → absent skip (no load). `wishlist.load` raising `ValueError` → same absent skip. wall_clock with no `sqlite_path` under that boom → `ok=True skipped_measurement=True skip_reason=acceptance_fixture_path_absent`.
- chosen_gate2_shape: (1) Resolver branches on identity: `config is None` → `wishlist.acceptance()`; any supplied mapping including `{}` is copied and used (no wishlist). Failures from `acceptance()` become `Refusal("ACCEPTANCE_CONFIG_UNREADABLE")` with `__cause__` chained. Do not wrap `wishlist.load()` globally. Do not catch-and-skip. Null key after a successful mapping remains absent; missing file remains missing. (2) Drain pin: amend the DEC-052 immutability test so post-entry admissible outcomes are a success dict with instrumentation or a measurement `Refusal` already raised by the measure body. Remove `ValueError`/`RuntimeError`/`OSError`/`sqlite3.Error` from the accepted tuple. Unexpected exceptions stay bugs. Do **not** convert generic drain exceptions into Refusal. Keep `drain_hits >= 1` and unmutated-bytes.
- proposed_gate1_contracts: New `tests/test_inc032_gate1_contracts.py`, expected-red against this Gate-0 tip: **c01** omitted config, patch `wishlist.acceptance` (not `load`) to raise; must not return an absent/missing skip tuple; admissible `Refusal("ACCEPTANCE_CONFIG_UNREADABLE")` with cause. **c02** wall_clock, no `sqlite_path`, omitted `acceptance_config`; same boom; must not return `ok=True` or `skipped_measurement=True`. **c03b** `config={}` is authoritative empty: absent skip and zero `acceptance`/`load` calls (today `{}` still loads wishlist). **c04** inspect/pin `test_dec052_measure_refresh_leaves_evidence_bytes_unchanged`: the post-entry tuple must not include those generic exception types (they are present today). Retained-green: **c03** `config={"fixture_sqlite_path": None}` still absent skip and does not call `acceptance`/`load`; existing DEC-052 skip test and `drain_hits >= 1`.
- scope_boundary: Gate 0 changed append-only bookkeeping only. It authorizes no Gate 1 test file, no `execution_benchmark.py` / wishlist / DEC-052-test production edit, no live catalog, INC-033, DEF-035/036, Gate 3, PR-state change, ready/merge, Fill, migration, drive, or synthesizing fixtures. Gate 1 requires separate authorization after review. Greptile omitted (DEC-062). Codex ×3: AMEND / AMEND / ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED.
- UPDATE 2026-08-20 — **INC-032 Gate 0 accepted at `663fc0c`.** Operator accept after Codex ×3 ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED. Inventory and Gate-2 shape stand. Gate 1 authorized.

### INC-036: Publication never unlinks the staging name, so a failed attempt cannot be retried
- date: 2026-08-21 / status: active — recorded for its own leftover-cleanup gate / triggered_by: INC-035 Gate 2 freeze at `4aa5550`; operator proceed / related: INC-035, INC-034, DEC-059, modelark/core/db.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- symptom: INC-035 opens `.catalog.sqlite.publish-staging` with `O_EXCL` and refuses that leftover pathname at the start of a later attempt. The staging name is never unlinked. After a failed publish the dest catalog is absent and the leftover name remains, so retry raises leftover-refuse. After a successful publish dest and staging share the inode (`nlink=2`).
- root_cause: INC-035 Gate 2 forbade pathname unlink because POSIX `unlink` is not inode-conditional; a check-then-unlink can delete a rival that swapped onto the name. That fail-closed rule is correct for dest safety and is now the retry blocker.
- blast_radius: Copies-only publication retry. Not live catalog. Dest bytes on the success path are the validated inode; the extra name is the problem, not dest clobber.
- why_not_caught_earlier: INC-035 Gate 1/2 pinned dest not receiving a swapped sentinel. A01 asserts staging absent only on a pre-staging sidecar refusal. No contract retries after a post-staging failure.
- boundary: Do not reopen INC-035 dest fd-link. No live catalog, Fill, drives, DEF-036, or Gate 3.

### INC-036 Gate-0 UPDATE: Unique staging-name retry inventory
- date: 2026-08-21 / status: Gate 0 inventory complete; pending reviewer/operator acceptance; Gate 1 not authorized / triggered_by: operator accept-and-proceed after INC-033/035 Gate 2 freeze at `4aa5550` / related: INC-036, INC-035, INC-034, modelark/core/db.py / docs_updated: docs/decision_log.md
- reproduced: at `4aa5550`, fixture publish `status=ok` leaves dest+staging same `(st_dev,st_ino)` with `nlink=2`. Same-dest retry refuses dest occupancy (not leftover). Staging-seam failure leaves dest absent and staging present (`nlink=1`); retry refuses leftover and does not publish. Planted leftover with dest absent is refused and preserved. Live catalog not opened.
- helper_inventory: Staging create is `os.open(..., O_RDWR|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_CLOEXEC|O_NOFOLLOW)` (`db.py` `publish_provenance_migration`). Leftover guard is `staging.exists()` immediately before that open. Publish is `_link_fd_no_clobber`. `finally` closes `stage_con` and `staging_fd` and does not unlink. No inode-conditional unlink helper exists. Python `os.unlink(name, dir_fd=)` / `unlinkat` still names the directory entry, not the inode.
- sibling_inventory: INC-034 unlinked the staging name after pathname `os.link`; INC-035 removed that unlink. INC-035 c01 still pins dest not receiving swapped sentinel bytes. A01 pins staging absent only when refusal happens before staging, but asserts the well-known name; unique names make that vacuous unless it snapshots `.catalog.sqlite.publish-staging*` and requires the set unchanged. Unique names also break hooks that hard-code `.catalog.sqlite.publish-staging`: INC-035 c01 and `test_publish_holds_source_lock_through_replace_blocks_writer` / `test_publish_staging_exclusive_lock_blocks_adversary_through_replace`. Gate 2 may adapt those three hooks plus A01 only to discover the active unique path from the staging fd (for example `/proc/self/fd/<fd>`) or an explicit test seam, and to snapshot the staging-name set; do not weaken, skip, or delete their assertions. Retain `test_m08c_existing_destination_refuses`.
- chosen_gate2_shape: **Never unlink a staging name** (INC-035 stands; POSIX unlinkat is not inode-conditional). Fix retry by stopping the single well-known staging pathname:
  1. Sole name source is a test-observable factory (e.g. `_publish_staging_path(dest_dir) -> Path`) returning `.catalog.sqlite.publish-staging.<token>` with a fresh collision-resistant token, not dest identity. Same `O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW` open as INC-035. Keep `_link_fd_no_clobber(staging_fd, dest_cat)` and `/proc/self/fd` + `AT_SYMLINK_FOLLOW`; do not switch to `AT_EMPTY_PATH`.
  2. Drop the start-of-publish `staging.exists()` refuse of `.catalog.sqlite.publish-staging`. `O_EXCL` on the unique path is the occupancy check. Do not glob-delete or unlink `publish-staging*` leftovers. Do **not** resample a new token in the same invocation on `EEXIST` or sidecar refuse — fail closed; a later invocation with a different factory result can succeed.
  3. Pre-dest-link failure: dest absent; that attempt's unique leftover may remain; the next attempt uses a new unique name and can publish.
  4. Success: dest is the validated inode (INC-034/035); an extra hardlink on the unique staging name may remain (`nlink>=2`). Same-dest retry still refuses dest occupancy before creating any new staging name.
  5. Sidecar refuse stays on dest_cat and on this attempt's staging path. Occupied unique main or `-wal`/`-shm`/`-journal` sidecar: refuse, dest absent, occupant `(st_dev,st_ino)`+bytes unchanged. No pathname `os.link`. No dest unlink. No live catalog.
- proposed_gate1_contracts: New `tests/test_inc036_gate1_contracts.py`, expected-red at `4aa5550`: **c01** first failure **before** `_link_fd_no_clobber` creates dest; dest absent and a staging leftover present; second publish without the injector must succeed **and the first leftover `(st_dev,st_ino)`+bytes must still exist after that success** (today leftover-refuse). **c02** dest absent plus a planted well-known leftover must still publish; snapshot `(st_dev,st_ino)`+bytes **before** the call and require the same inode (path must not disappear or be replaced) through success (today leftover-refuse). **c04** factory yields `[occupied_unique_main, free_path]` across two invocations: first call-count is exactly 1, refuse, dest absent, free path untouched, occupant inode+bytes unchanged; second invocation makes exactly the next factory call, publishes via `free_path`, first occupant still unchanged (today no factory). **c05** same two-invocation factory sequence parametrized for occupied `-wal`/`-shm`/`-journal` with unique main absent (today no factory / well-known leftover guard). Retained-green **c03**: after fd-link, swap staging name to sentinel; snapshot sentinel `(st_dev,st_ino)`+bytes for the whole remaining publish; dest is not sentinel. Retained-green **c06**: successful publish then same-dest retry refuses dest occupancy; dest inode+bytes exact; the `.catalog.sqlite.publish-staging*` set after the first success is unchanged (second invocation creates no additional staging main/sidecar). Retained-green: INC-035 c01, INC-034 c01, A01–A03 (A01 staging-set snapshot), m08c, and the two lock-boundary tests (adapt path discovery only). Post-link failure must not require dest overwrite (INC-034).
- scope_boundary: Gate 0 changed append-only bookkeeping only. It authorizes no Gate 1 test file, no `db.py` production, live catalog, DEF-036, Gate 3, ready/merge, Fill, repair, or drives. Greptile omitted (DEC-062). Round 1 Codex ×3: REJECT / ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED / AMEND (applied). Round 2 ×3: pass 1 **ACCEPT**; pass 2 **AMEND** (applied); pass 3 **AMEND** (applied: c04/c05 two-invocation factory sequence, exactly-once call, later invocation recovers). Round 2 did **not** land a third-pass ACCEPT / ACCEPT-AS-AMENDED.
- UPDATE 2026-08-21 — **INC-036 Gate 0 accepted at `0a50f35`.** Operator accept. Operator-scratch markdown moved into gitignored `claudedocs/operator-scratch/` (do not write `~/PycharmProjects/modelark-*.md`). Gate 1 authorized. Live catalog still off-limits.
